Trigger on: /meta:socratic, 'check your assumptions', 'are you sure', 'best case for', 'strongest argument', 'play devil's advocate', 'what if they're right'. Do NOT activate for routine coding, small fixes, or tasks where fast failure is cheaper than analysis.
